- 170 -
 WP No. 104848 of 2021
 and connected matters
THESE PETITIONS COMING ON FOR ORDERS THIS DAY,
THE COURT MADE THE FOLLOWING:
ORDER
The learned Additional Government Advocate accepts
notice for respondent Nos.1 to 3.
2. The learned Additional Government Advocate
has raised a preliminary objections with regard to
maintainability of the writ petitions on the ground that the
petitioners in the above petitions are working as plantation
watchers with the respondent No.3, and hence, the
petitioners, being civil servants, are required to approach
the Karnataka Administrative Tribunal for redressal of their
grievances.
3. The learned counsel for the petitioners, after
arguing the matter for some time, seeks leave of this court
to withdraw the writ petitions with liberty to approach the
Karnataka Administrative Tribunal for redressal of their
grievances.

- 171 -
 WP No. 104848 of 2021
 and connected matters
4. Accordingly, the petitions are dismissed as
withdrawn reserving liberty as sought for. All contentions
raised in these petitions are kept open.
Sd/-
JUDGE
